SOV_five_year_plan_dec_cat = {

	SOV_five_year_plan_decision = {

		icon = GFX_SOV_tractor_factories

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		visible = {
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = SOV_finish_the_five_year_plan
			}
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = five_year_plan_decision_limit_tt
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_ukraine_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_moscow_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_urals_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_siberia_rng
				}
				check_variable = { SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions < 3 }
			}
		}

		days_re_enable = 100

		complete_effect = {

			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = 0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_ukraine_rng
				}

				random_list = {

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
									free_building_slots = { 
										building = industrial_complex 
										size > 0
									}
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}

					30 = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {

				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_moscow_rng
				}

				random_list = {

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_moscow = yes
									free_building_slots = { 
										building = industrial_complex 
										size > 0
									}
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_moscow = yes
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}

					30 = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_moscow = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {

				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_urals_rng
				}

				random_list = {

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_urals = yes
									free_building_slots = { 
										building = industrial_complex 
										size > 0
									}
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_urals = yes
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}

					30 = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_urals = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {

				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_siberia_rng
				}

				random_list = {

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_siberia = yes
									free_building_slots = { 
										building = industrial_complex 
										size > 0
									}
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_siberia = yes
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}

					30 = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = industrial_complex
									size > 0
								}
								infrastructure > 5
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_siberia = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
							set_variable = { pre_mission_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			activate_mission = SOV_five_year_plan_mission
		}
	}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_8
							set_variable = { pre_mission_refineries = building_level@synthetic_refinery }
						}
					}

					30 = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								free_building_slots = { 
									building = synthetic_refinery 
									size > 0
								}
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_moscow = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_8
							set_variable = { pre_mission_refineries = building_level@synthetic_refinery }
						}
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {

				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_urals_rng
				}

				random_list = {

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_urals = yes
									free_building_slots = { 
										building = synthetic_refinery 
										size > 0
									}
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_urals = yes
								free_building_slots = { 
									building = synthetic_refinery 
									size > 0
								}
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_8

	SOV_five_year_plan_decision_9 = {

		icon = GFX_SOV_tractor_factories

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		visible = {
			has_war = no
			has_completed_focus = SOV_finish_the_five_year_plan
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = five_year_plan_decision_limit_tt
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_ukraine_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_moscow_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_urals_rng
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_siberia_rng
				}
				check_variable = { SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions < 3 }
			}
		}

		days_re_enable = 100

		complete_effect = {

			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = 0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_gosplan_ukraine_rng
				}

					70 = {

						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_owned_state = {
									is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
									infrastructure < 8
									NOT = { 
										has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
								infrastructure < 8
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
							set_variable = { pre_mission_infrastructure = building_level@infrastructure }
							add_to_variable = { pre_mission_infrastructure = 2 }
						}
					}

					30 = {

							limit = {
								infrastructure < 8
								NOT = {
									has_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
								}
								NOT = {
									is_gosplan_ukraine = yes
								}
							}

							set_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
							set_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
							set_variable = { pre_mission_infrastructure = building_level@infrastructure }
							add_to_variable = { pre_mission_infrastructure = 2 }

	SOV_five_year_plan_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				# Makes the map glow wherever this flag is present
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
			}
			highlight_color_while_active = 3 # Color code 3 is Green
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		is_good = no

		days_mission_timeout = 90

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission

				check_variable = {
					var = pre_mission_civs
					value = building_level@industrial_complex
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_factories
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}

					clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
					clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
					clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}

					clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
					clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
					clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_progression
				}

				add_to_variable = { SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 4 }
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
				}
				add_to_variable = { SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 3 }
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
				add_to_variable = { 
					SOV_fyp_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0.01
					tooltip = production_speed_buildings_factor_tt
				}
			}
		}

		timeout_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			### punishment here
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
				}

				clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
				clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission
				clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}
		}
	}

					has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_2
				}

				clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
				clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_2
				clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_five_year_plan_mission_3 = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				# Makes the map glow wherever this flag is present
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
			}
			highlight_color_while_active = 3 # Color code 3 is Green
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		is_good = no

		days_mission_timeout = 90

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3

				check_variable = {
					var = pre_mission_civs
					value = building_level@industrial_complex
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_factories
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}

					clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
					clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
					clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
				}
			}
			
			else = {
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}

					clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
					clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
					clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_progression
				}

				add_to_variable = { SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 4 }
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
				}
				add_to_variable = { SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 3 }
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dynamic_modifier = SOV_second_five_year_plan_dynamic_modifier
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
				add_to_variable = { 
					SOV_fyp_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0.01
					tooltip = production_speed_buildings_factor_tt
				}
			}
		}

		timeout_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			### punishment here
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
				}

				clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
				clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_3
				clear_variable = pre_mission_civs
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_five_year_plan_mission_4 = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				# Makes the map glow wherever this flag is present
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_4
			}
			highlight_color_while_active = 3 # Color code 3 is Green
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		is_good = no

		days_mission_timeout = 90

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_4

				check_variable = {
					var = pre_mission_infrastructure
					value = building_level@infrastructure
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_progression
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_4
					}
					random_list = {
						16 = {
	   						modifier = {
								factor = 3
								oil > 0
							}
							add_resource = {
								type = oil
								amount = 12
							}
						}
						16 = {
						  	modifier = {
								factor = 3
								steel > 0
							}
	   						add_resource = {
								type = steel
								amount = 16
							}
   						}
						16 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 3
								aluminium > 0
							}
							add_resource = {
								type = aluminium
								amount = 16
							}
   						}
   						16 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 3
								tungsten > 0
							}
							add_resource = {
								type = tungsten
								amount = 8
							}
   						}
   						16 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 3
								chromium > 0
							}
	 						add_resource = {
								type = chromium
								amount = 8
							}
						}
   					 	16 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 3
								rubber > 0
							}
  							add_resource = {
								type = rubber
								amount = 8
							}
   						}
					}

					clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
					clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_4
					clear_variable = pre_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_focus_on_factories
				}
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_4
					}
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
					random_list = {
						16 = {
	   						modifier = {
								factor = 3
								oil > 0
							}
							add_resource = {
								type = oil
								amount = 6
							}
						}
						16 = {
						  	modifier = {
								factor = 3
								steel > 0
							}
	   						add_resource = {
								type = steel
								amount = 8

   						}
						16 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 3
								aluminium > 0
							}
							add_resource = {

   						}
   						16 = {

							add_resource = {
								type = tungsten

   						}
   						16 = {

					has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
				}
				random_list = {
					16 = {
	   					modifier = {
							factor = 3
							oil > 0
						}
						add_resource = {
							type = oil
							amount = 6
						}
					}
					16 = {
					  	modifier = {
							factor = 3
							steel > 0
						}
	   					add_resource = {
							type = steel
							amount = 8
						}
   					}
					16 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 3
							aluminium > 0
						}
						add_resource = {
							type = aluminium
							amount = 8
						}
   					}
   					16 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 3
							tungsten > 0
						}
						add_resource = {
							type = tungsten
							amount = 4
						}
   					}
   					16 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 3
							chromium > 0
						}
	 					add_resource = {
							type = chromium
							amount = 4
							}
					}
   					16 = {
						modifier = {
							factor = 3
							rubber > 0
						}
  						add_resource = {
							type = rubber
							amount = 4
						}
   					}
				}
				clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
				clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
				clear_variable = pre_mission_infrastructure
			}

			add_to_variable = { SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 2 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}
		}

		timeout_effect = {

			subtract_from_variable = { 
				SOV_active_five_year_plan_missions = 1
			}

			### punishment here
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
				}

				clr_state_flag = SOV_five_year_plan_active_state
				clr_state_flag = SOV_flag_for_five_year_plan_mission_9
				clear_variable = pre_mission_infrastructure
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_modify_fyp_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = { 
				SOV_fyp_consumer_goods_expected_value = -0.01
				tooltip = consumer_goods_expected_value_tt
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_five_year_plan_extend_deadline = {

		icon = generic_political_actions

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		visible = {
			has_active_mission = SOV_second_five_year_plan_master_mission
		}

		cost = 50 

		days_re_enable = 100

		complete_effect = {

			add_stability = -0.03

			add_days_mission_timeout = {
				mission = SOV_second_five_year_plan_master_mission
				days = 30
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_second_five_year_plan_master_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		is_good = no

		days_mission_timeout = 730

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SOV_five_year_plan_mission_tt
				check_variable = {
					var = SOV_five_year_plan_progress
					value = 100
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { SOV_accumulated_factories > 0 }
				}
				while_loop_effect = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { SOV_accumulated_factories > 0 }
					}

					random_owned_controlled_state = {
						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1 
						add_building_construction = {
							type = industrial_complex
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
					subtract_from_variable = { SOV_accumulated_factories = 1 }
				}
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_five_year_plan_reward_tt

			SOV = {
				complete_national_focus = SOV_finish_the_five_year_plan
				custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
				complete_national_focus = SOV_third_five_year_plan
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_fyp_set_construction_master_mission_tt
			set_variable = {
				SOV_fyp_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0.05
				tooltip = production_speed_buildings_factor_tt
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt

			set_variable = {
				SOV_five_year_plan_progress = 0
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { SOV_temp_cg_duration < 100 }
				}
				set_variable = {
					SOV_temp_cg_duration = 100
				}
			}

			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = SOV_second_five_year_plan_master_mission_reward
				days = SOV_temp_cg_duration
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			activate_mission = SOV_third_five_year_plan_master_mission

			clear_variable = SOV_temp_cg_duration
		}

		timeout_effect = {
			### punishment here
			SOV = {
				complete_national_focus = SOV_finish_the_five_year_plan
				complete_national_focus = SOV_third_five_year_plan
			}

			activate_mission = SOV_third_five_year_plan_master_mission
		}
	}

	SOV_third_five_year_plan_master_mission = {

		}

		visible = {
			has_country_flag = SOV_siberia_gosplan
		}

		available = {
			has_country_flag = SOV_siberia_gosplan_1
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 120

		days_remove = 90

		modifier = {
			political_power_factor = -0.1
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_ideas = { SOV_gosplan_siberia_spirit }
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			clr_country_flag = SOV_siberia_gosplan_1
		}
		
		remove_effect = {
			random_owned_state = {
				prioritize = { 408 }
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 6
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
				}

				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = SOV_siberia_gosplan_2
		}
	}

	SOV_leningrad_conversion = {

		icon = GFX_SOV_tractor_factories

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 195
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		available = {
			195 = {
				has_state_flag = SOV_dual_factories
			}
			has_war = yes
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 50

		complete_effect = {
			195 = {
				remove_dynamic_modifier = {
					modifier = SOV_dual_factories_dynamic_modifiers
					scope = SOV
				}
				
				clr_state_flag = SOV_dual_factories

				set_variable = { current_civs = building_level@industrial_complex }
				set_variable = { eligible_civs = current_civs }
				subtract_from_variable = { eligible_civs = pre_dual_use_civs }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { eligible_civs > 0 }
					}
					while_loop_effect = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { eligible_civs > 0 }
						}
						remove_building = {
							type = industrial_complex
							level = 1
							instant_build = yes
						}

						add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
						add_building_construction = {
							type = arms_factory
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
						subtract_from_variable = { eligible_civs = 1}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

SOV_industrial_evacuation_dec_cat = {

	SOV_the_question_of_industrial_evacuation = {
		icon = GFX_SOV_tractor_factories

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		visible = {
			date > 1940.6.1
		}

		complete_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = soviet.new.evacuations
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_evacuation_192 = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 192
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		visible = {
			has_war = yes
			192 = {
				is_controlled_by = SOV

				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_evacuation_disrupted
				}

				OR = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						NOT = {
							is_controlled_by = SOV
						}
					}
					any_neighbor_state = {
						any_neighbor_state = {
							NOT = { 
								is_controlled_by = SOV
							}
						}
					}
				}

				OR = {
					industrial_complex > 0
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_192_mission

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_cost
				producer = SOV
			}
		}
	}

	SOV_evacuation_192_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 192
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		days_mission_timeout = SOV_evacuation_days

		available = {
			192 = {
				NOT = {
					is_controlled_by = SOV
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_refund
				producer = SOV
			}
			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					192 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				192 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					192 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				192 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					192 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				192 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}

	SOV_evacuation_89_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {

				state = 89
			}
		}

		}

		activation = {

		}

					is_controlled_by = SOV
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_refund
				producer = SOV
			}
			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					89 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				89 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					89 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				89 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}

					}
				}
			}

				limit = {
					89 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				89 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {

							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {

					89 = {
						arms_factory > 1
					}
				}

				89 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {

							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {

							size > 1

					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {

					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					89 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				89 = {
					remove_building = {

						level = 1
					}

							state = 882

	SOV_evacuation_198 = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 198
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		visible = {
			has_war = yes
			198 = {
				is_controlled_by = SOV

				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_evacuation_disrupted
				}

				OR = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						NOT = {
							is_controlled_by = SOV
						}
					}
					any_neighbor_state = {
						any_neighbor_state = {
							NOT = {
								is_controlled_by = SOV
							}
						}
					}
				}

				OR = {
					industrial_complex > 0
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_198_mission

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_cost
				producer = SOV
			}
		}
	}

		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					201 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				201 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations

		visible = {
			has_war = yes
			202 = {
				is_controlled_by = SOV

				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_evacuation_disrupted
				}

				OR = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						NOT = {
							is_controlled_by = SOV
						}
					}
					any_neighbor_state = {
						any_neighbor_state = {
							NOT = {
								is_controlled_by = SOV
							}
						}
					}
				}

				OR = {
					industrial_complex > 0
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_202_mission

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment

	SOV_evacuation_202_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 202
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		days_mission_timeout = SOV_evacuation_days

		available = {
			202 = {
				NOT = {
					is_controlled_by = SOV
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_refund
				producer = SOV
			}
			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					202 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					202 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					202 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					202 = {
						arms_factory > 1
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					202 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					202 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				202 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					202 = {
						OR = {
							industrial_complex > 0
							arms_factory > 0
						}
				 		is_controlled_by = SOV
					}
				}
				activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_202_mission
			}

			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_full_refund
					producer = SOV
				}
			}
		}

		}

				}

					any_neighbor_state = {

						}
					}
					any_neighbor_state = {

							}

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 228
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		visible = {
			has_war = yes
			228 = {
				is_controlled_by = SOV

				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = SOV_evacuation_disrupted
				}

				OR = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						NOT = {
							is_controlled_by = SOV
						}
					}
					any_neighbor_state = {
						any_neighbor_state = {
							NOT = {
								is_controlled_by = SOV
							}
						}
					}
				}

				OR = {
					industrial_complex > 0
					arms_factory > 0
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_228_mission

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_cost
				producer = SOV

		}

	SOV_evacuation_228_mission = {

		icon = generic_construction

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 228
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = SOV
		}

		activation = {
			always = no
		}

		days_mission_timeout = SOV_evacuation_days

		available = {
			228 = {
				NOT = {
					is_controlled_by = SOV
				}
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment
				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_refund
				producer = SOV
			}
			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					228 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					228 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					228 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					228 = {
						arms_factory > 1
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					228 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					228 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				228 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					228 = {
						OR = {
							industrial_complex > 0
							arms_factory > 0
						}
				 		is_controlled_by = SOV
					}
				}
				activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_228_mission
			}

			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_full_refund
					producer = SOV
				}
			}
		}

				amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_refund
				producer = SOV

			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					253 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				253 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					253 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}

			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					238 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				238 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					238 = {

				producer = SOV
			}
			add_stability = -0.003
		}
	
		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					233 = {
						industrial_complex > 1
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					233 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					233 = {
						industrial_complex > 0
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = industrial_complex
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					233 = {
						arms_factory > 1
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 1
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = SOV_double_evacuations
					233 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			else_if = {
				limit = {
					233 = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
				}

				233 = {
					remove_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
					}
				}

				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 583
							state = 588
							state = 590
							state = 882
						}
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
						}
					}

					add_building_construction = {
						type = arms_factory
						level = 1
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					233 = {
						OR = {
							industrial_complex > 0
							arms_factory > 0
						}
				 		is_controlled_by = SOV
					}
				}
				activate_mission = SOV_evacuation_233_mission
			}

			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = SOV_evacuation_trains_full_refund
					producer = SOV
				}
			}
		}
	}
